_source line 3 · 2026-05-15T13:13:43.133Z_

[Director 指令] [Director 指令] 审稿等待期实验——占满 2 GPU

审稿预计 5/17-19 返回，之前 2 张卡不能空着。以下两组实验并行：

**GPU0: plan-008 Router Size Ablation（P1, ~12 GPU-hours）**
对应 C2 "lightweight router" claim。用 plan-001b 的 245 episodes 训练数据，对比三个 router：
- Qwen3-1.7B（当前 SimGate router）
- Qwen2.5-1.5B（更小）
- Qwen2.5-3B（更大）
训练配置与现有 router 完全一致（lr=2e-5, batch=32, epochs=3, max_seq_len=2048）。只改 model checkpoint。
评估指标：balanced accuracy + internal recall + eval loss（logit-level 即可，不需要 generation eval）。
如果 1.5B ≈ 1.7B → "small router suffices"；如果 3B >> 1.7B → 需讨论 capacity-performance tradeoff。

**GPU1: 核心结果 Seed Robustness（~8 GPU-hours）**
论文核心数字（plan-012 的 Δ_full=29.6, DAF=90.41%, r=0.9698）目前只有 1 个 seed。跑 3 个额外 seed（不同 random seed 划分 train/val split），报告 mean ± std。
目标：Δ_full, Δ_nd, DAF, Pearson r 四个指标的 cross-seed 稳定性。如果 std 小，论文可以从 "29.6" 升级为 "29.6 ± X"，增强可信度。

两组实验独立无依赖，立即并行启动。注册 registry 时标注 plan_id。完成后汇报。

\"notes\": \"Generation-based routing eval 推翻了此前 logit-level 的 success 结论。\\n\\n**Logit-level (Trainer eval, 训练时)**: accuracy=74.5%, balanced_acc=79.1%, internal_recall=84.9% — 看起来成功。\\n**Generation-based eval**: accuracy=53.3%, balanced_acc=41.5%, internal_recall=26.7% — 实际失败。\\n\\ninternal_recall=26.7% < 30% 判定线 → conclusion 改为 negative。\\n\\n三种 thinking 模式（thinking_on, thinking_off, thinking_on+strip）结果完全一致，说明 thinking mode 不是根因。\\n\\n与 ablation-b 对比: accuracy 53.3% vs 65.4%, balanced_acc 41.5% vs 59.3%, internal_recall 26.7% vs 51.5%。全面落后。\\n\\n与 plan-002-generative-v1 同样的问题模式：logit-level 指标与 generation-level 指标严重不一致。模型在 next-token prediction (loss) 层面学会了区分路由，但在自回归生成时无法可靠地输出 [SIMULATE] 前缀。\\n\\n根因推测：causal LM 的 generation 和 teacher-forcing eval 是不同的推理模式。训练时看到完整序列可以区分路由，但生成时第一个 token 的选择（是否输出 \\\"[\\\" 开始 [SIMULATE]）不够确定。\"}, \"plan-003-baseline-245eps\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T08:11:02.369Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T08:11:34.208Z\", \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"notes\": \"245 eps baseline results: always-int=-39.07 [-44.89,-33.37] death=42.9%; always-det=-9.47 [-13.66,-5.11] data/plan-004-implicit (2040) | results/plan_c_test_labels (1814) | data/plan-004-v3 (2040, **=ablation-b**) |\\n| upsample_minority | **true** | false | false |\\n| class_weights | 无 | \\\"auto\\\" | 48.15 | 48.15 (用户报告) |\\n| Epoch 1 eval_loss | ~0.68 | 0.95 | [无 train.log] |\\n| Epoch 3 eval_loss | 0.36 | **0.49 (收敛了!)** | [无 train.log] |\\n| 平均 train_loss | 1.26 | 12.74 | [无数据] |\\n\\n**重要发现: v2 虽然起步 loss=48，但最终 eval_loss 收敛到 0.49，模型实际上学到了。**\\n\\n### class_weights 代码分析\\nclass_weights 在 WeightedTrainer.compute_loss() 中正确实现：\\n- sample_idx 由 ImplicitSFTDataset.__getitem__() 返回 (line 85)\\n- PaddingCollator.__call__() 正确传递 (line 128-136)\\n- compute_loss() 中 `inputs.pop(\\\"sample_idx\\\")` 获取，条件 `self._class_weights is not None and sample_indices is not None` 控制 (line 227)\\n- v2 (class_weights=\\\"auto\\\"): 权重 internal=0.743, det=1.527，正确计算并应用\\n- v3 (class_weights=null): 无权重，`loss = per_sample_loss.mean()`\\n\\n**class_weights 代码本身没有 bug。v2 和 v3 的 step 10 loss 一致（48.15），证明 class_weights 不是 loss 差异的主因。** class_weights 只是对 per-sample loss 的乘法缩放（0.74x~1.53x），不会改变 loss 的数量级。\\n\\n### plan-002-formal-v6 数据路径\\n- 数据来源: `results/plan_c/` 目录（11 个 task_type 子目录的 labeled_steps.jsonl）\\n- 加载: 2040 samples from 11 files\\n- 标签分布: deterministic 1830 (89.7%), internal 210 (10.3%)\\n- 与 ablation-b 的 implicit 数据是**同源数据，但格式不同**（explicit router 用 observation+action_history+label 格式，implicit 用 messages chat 格式）\\n- **plan-002-formal-v6 也退化了**: 全预测 deterministic (internal: 0/30, deterministic: 390/390)\\n\\n### 结论：根因判定\\n\\n**\\\"数据文件不同\\\"假说被证伪（对 v3）**：v3 使用和 ablation-b 完全相同的数据（MD5 一致），但仍然异常。\\n\\n**真正的变量是训练策略的组合**：\\n1. **`upsample_minority: true`**（ablation-b 独有）：在 89.7% deterministic 的极端不平衡数据上，WeightedRandomSampler 确保 internal 样本被充分采样。没有它，模型每 batch 几乎只看到 deterministic 样本。\\n2. **`gradient_accumulation_steps: 1 vs 8`**：有效 batch 4 vs 32。较大 batch + 无上采样 = 每步看到的 internal 样本更少。\\n\\n**v2 的数据确实不同**（标签比例反转：internal 67% vs 10%，只有 4 个 task_type），但 v2 依然收敛了（eval_loss 0.49）。这说明数据差异导致训练行为不同，但不是致命问题。\\n\\n**建议下一步**：\\n- 用 ablation-b 的配置（upsample_minority=true, gradient_accumulation=1）重跑 v3 数据，验证是否恢复正常 loss\\n- 对 v2 跑 eval 看是否也退化\"}, \"plan-004-v2\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T06:41:13.286Z\", \"updated_at\": <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01FdoR1W18Te1oaciQDuw5Cb`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

- SSH to westb-13313
- Check existing code: `src/training/implicit_router_sft.py` (the generative SFT router)

- Train each model using the same hyperparameters: learning rate 2e-5, batch size 4, gradient accumulation 8, 3 epochs, 2048 max sequence length with minority class upsampling, then evaluate using balanced accuracy, internal recall, and eval loss at the logit level

I need to double-check the batch size strategy against plan-008's requirement for an effective batch of 32. The Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen2.5-1.5B should work fine with batch_size=4 and ga=8, but the larger Qwen2.5-3B might need adjustment depending on GPU memory, so I'll let the training script handle that dynamically.

Looking back at the actual training script being used — the current setup runs `implicit_router_sft.py` for the SimGate router, which trains the model to output a [SIMULATE] token rather than using a separate classification module. But the Director's comparison mentions three explicit routers, so I need to verify whether plan-008 should stick with the implicit approach or switch to explicit routing with `router_sft_train.py`.

The key issue is that swapping base models (Qwen3-1.7B to Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B) means different tokenizers and architectures, so I should just give the Worker clear instructions and let it handle the implementation details.

For the seed robustness work, I'm planning to retrain the router three times with different seeds to get different train/val splits, then report balanced accuracy, internal recall, and eval loss for each. I'll also run bootstrap resampling on the 245 episodes to compute confidence intervals for the key metrics and report means with standard deviations. The ~8 GPU-hours estimate comes from three router trainings at roughly 2-2.5 hours each.

Now I need to register these experiments and spawn the workers. Since the Director already gave approval with "立即并行启动", I can move directly to the PREPARING state without waiting for additional sign-off. I need to follow the protocol: register the experiments, spawn workers, report completion to the Director, then end the conversation. But I'm realizing the seed robustness task might be more subtle—the Director wants mean ± std across 3 seeds, but metrics like Δ_full and DAF are computed deterministically on the full 245-episode dataset, so they shouldn't vary with train/val split seeds unless the environment itself has randomness that propagates through the evaluation.

Given the ~8 GPU-hour budget, I think the right approach is to train the router 3 times with different seeds for the train/val split, then use bootstrap confidence intervals on the decomposition metrics to show stability. This gives the Director evidence of metric robustness even if the mechanism differs slightly from what they initially envisioned.

Now I need to check the existing ablation-b training command to understand the exact format before spawning the workers.

Setting up the training command with the same structure as ablation-b, but I'll swap in the plan-008 model and output directories, then run it three times with different seeds to establish robustness across the train/val splits.

确认 conda 环境可用\n   - 确认训练脚本存在：`ls src/training/implicit_router_sft.py`\n   - 确认数据存在：`ls data/plan-004-implicit/`\n   - 检查 3 个模型是否都已下载。缺失的先下载（用 ModelScope，AutoDL 上 HF 下载极慢）\n\n2. **先读一下 `implicit_router_sft.py` 的参数**，确认它支持 `--model_name` 指定不同模型。注意：\n   - Qwen3-1.7B 是 Qwen3 系列（可能有 thinking mode）\n   - Qwen2.5-1.5B / 3B 是 Qwen2.5 系列（无 thinking mode）\n   - 确认脚本对不同模型架构兼容\n\n3. **写一个 wrapper 脚本** `scripts/plan008_router_size_ablation.py` 或 `.sh`，按顺序训练 3 个模型：\n   - 每个模型训练完后保存到不同 output_dir：\n     - `checkpoints/plan-008-qwen3-1.7b/`\n     - `checkpoints/plan-008-qwen2.5-1.5b/`\n     - `checkpoints/plan-008-qwen2.5-3b/`\n   - 每个模型训练完后立即 eval（logit-level），记录 balanced accuracy, internal recall, eval loss\n   - 脚本自动汇总结果到 `results/plan-008/summary.json`\n\n4. **Dry-run 验证**：先用 epochs=1、每个模型只跑几步确认不报错\n\n5. **用 `submit_training_job` 提交正式训练**。submit 的 command 应包含完整环境激活：\n   ```\n   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing && python scripts/plan008_router_size_ablation.py\n   ```\n\n6. **更新 registry**：\n   - 转 RUNNING 时带 spec（command, server, data_path, data_count, model_name, seed）\n   - exp_id: `plan-008-router-size`\n   - plan_id: `plan-008`\n\n### 注意事项\n- **GPU 编号从 0 开始**：`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`（框架自动映射）\n- Qwen2.5-3B 可能需要更多显存，如果 batch_size=4 OOM，降到 batch_size=2 + ga=16（保持 effective batch=32）。在脚本中做好 OOM fallback\n- RTX 5090 有 32GB 显存\n- 训练日志保存到文件，方便后续分析\n- 如果模型下载需要很长时间，可以先下载模型，再写训练脚本\n\n### 评估指标（logit-level 即可，不需要 generation eval）\n- balanced accuracy\n- internal recall\n- eval loss\n- per-class precision/recall/f1\n\n### 产出\n- `results/plan-008/summary.json`：三个模型的对比结果\n- 各模型 checkpoint 目录\n- 训练日志",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：核心结果 Seed Robustness（GPU1）\n\n在 westb-13313 服务器上，用 GPU1 验证论文核心数字的 cross-seed 稳定性。\n\n### 背景\n论文核心数字（plan-012: Δ_full=29.6, DAF=90.41%, r=0.9698）和 router diagnostic（balanced_acc=58.1%）目前主要基于 seed=42。需要 3 个额外 seed 证明稳定性。\n\n### 两部分工作\n\n#### Part A: Router 训练 multi-seed（~6-8 GPU-hours）\n用 3 个不同 seed（43, 44, 45）重训 router。每个 seed 会产生不同 train/val split，从而得到不同 balanced accuracy。\n\n**已有 seed=42 结果**（plan-013/ablation-b）：\n- balanced_acc = 0.5761\n- internal_recall = 0.5152（来自 plan-004-v5 数据，ablation-b 可能不同）\n- eval_loss ≈ 0.24\n\n**训练脚本**：`src/training/implicit_router_sft.py`\n**训练数据**：`data/plan-004-implicit/`（2040 samples）\n**配置**：lr=2e-5, batch_size=4, ga=8, epochs=3, upsample_minority, seed={43,44,45}\n\n**重要**：先读 `implicit_router_sft.py` 检查 --seed 参数是否控制 train/val split。如果 data 目录里已经是 pre-split 的 train.jsonl/val.jsonl，那 --seed 不会改变 split。这种情况需要：\n1. 找到原始数据准备脚本（可能在 `src/` 或 `scripts/` 下）\n2. 用不同 seed 重新准备数据\n3. 或在训练脚本中添加按 seed re-split 的逻辑\n\n**参考命令**：\n```bash\nCUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 WANDB_MODE=offline HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1 python src/training/implicit_router_sft.py \\\n  --data_dir data/plan-004-implicit \\\n  --model_name /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B \\\n  --epochs 3 \\\n  --batch_size 4 \\\n  --gradient_accumulation_steps 8 \\\n  --upsample_minority \\\n  --lr 2e-5 \\\n  --output_dir checkpoints/seed-robustness-seed{N} \\\n  --seed {N}\n```\n\n#### Part B: Decomposition metrics bootstrap（纯分析，几分钟）\nplan-012 的 Δ_full, Δ_nd, DAF, Pearson r 是在全部 245 episodes 上确定性计算的，没有随机性。为证明稳定性，用 bootstrap 重采样：\n1. 从 245 episodes 中有放回抽样 245 个，重计算 Δ_full, Δ_nd, DAF, r\n2. 重复 1000 次\n3. 报告 mean ± std 和 95% CI\n\n**数据路径**：`results/plan_c/`（245 episodes, 11 tasks）\n**分析脚本参考**：`src/death_decomposition.py`\n\n也可以做 leave-one-task-out jackknife 分析（每次去掉一个 task，计算剩余 10 tasks 的 metrics），验证无单一 task 主导结果。\n\n### 执行步骤\n\n1. **SSH 到服务器，检查环境**\n\n2. **检查数据和脚本**：\n   - `ls data/plan-004-implicit/` — 查看是 pre-split 还是需要 split\n   - `head -5 data/plan-004-implicit/*.jsonl` — 看数据格式\n   - 读 `implicit_router_sft.py` 的 split 逻辑\n\n3. **写 wrapper 脚本** `scripts/seed_robustness.py`：\n   - Part A: 按顺序用 seed 43, 44, 45 训练 3 个 router\n   - 每个训练完后 eval，记录 metrics\n   - Part B: bootstrap 分析\n   - 汇总所有结果到 `results/seed-robustness/summary.json`\n   - 格式：\n     ```json\n     {\n       \"router_metrics\": {\n         \"seeds\": [42, 43, 44, 45],\n         \"balanced_acc\": [0.5761, ..., ..., ...],\n         \"internal_recall\": [...],\n         \"eval_loss\": [...],\n         \"mean_balanced_acc\": ...,\n         \"std_balanced_acc\": ...\n       },\n       \"decomposition_bootstrap\": {\n         \"n_bootstrap\": 1000,\n         \"delta_full\": {\"mean\": ..., \"std\": ..., \"ci95\": [...]},\n         \"delta_nd\": {\"mean\": ..., \"std\": ..., \"ci95\": [...]},\n         \"daf\": {\"mean\": ..., \"std\": ..., \"ci95\": [...]},\n         \"pearson_r\": {\"mean\": ..., \"std\": ..., \"ci95\": [...]}\n       }\n     }\n     ```\n\n4. **Dry-run 验证**：先用 epochs=1 + 1 个 seed 确认不报错\n\n5. **用 `submit_training_job` 提交正式训练**。command 包含环境激活：\n   ```\n   source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing && python scripts/seed_robustness.py\n   ```\n\n6. **更新 registry**：\n   - {

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断 seed-robustness-core 实验结果\n\nseed-robustness-core 训练进程（PID 474403）已在 westb-13313 退出，cron 无法自动判断成功/失败。你需要：\n\n### 1. 检查日志和结果\n\nSSH 到 westb-13313，检查以下内容：\n\n```bash\n# 查看训练日志最后 50 行\ntail -50 /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/seed-robustness/run.log\n\n# 检查各 seed 的结果文件是否存在\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/seed-robustness/\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/seed-robustness/router_seed*.json\n\n# 检查汇总文件\ncat /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/results/seed-robustness/summary.json\n\n# 检查各 seed 的 checkpoint 是否存在\nls -la /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/checkpoints/seed-robustness-seed*/\n```\n\n### 2. 判断结果\n\n**成功条件**：3 个 seed (43,44,45) 都训练完成，每个都有 eval metrics（balanced_acc, internal_recall, eval_loss）\n\n**如果成功**：\n- `update_registry(exp_id=\"seed-robustness-core\", field=\"metrics\", value=...)` — 合并 router training 结果到 metrics（保留已有的 decomposition_bootstrap 和 jackknife 数据，加入 router_metrics）\n  - 格式：`router_metrics: {seeds: [42,43,44,45], balanced_acc: [...], internal_recall: [...], eval_loss: [...], mean_balanced_acc: ..., std_balanced_acc: ..., mean_internal_recall: ..., std_internal_recall: ...}`\n  - seed=42 的数据：balanced_acc=0.5927, internal_recall=0.5152, eval_loss=0.2382（来自 plan-004-ablation-b）\n- `update_registry(exp_id=\"seed-robustness-core\", field=\"conclusion\", value=\"success\")`\n- `update_registry(exp_id=\"seed-robustness-core\", field=\"status\", value=\"DONE\")`\n\n**如果失败**（某些 seed 没跑完或报错）：\n- 记录失败原因到 registry notes\n- `update_registry(exp_id=\"seed-robustness-core\", field=\"conclusion\", value=\"failed\")`\n- `update_registry(exp_id=\"seed-robustness-core\", field=\"status\", value=\"DONE\")`\n\n### 3. 清除 cron\n无论成功失败，都调用 `delete_cron(\"seed-robustness-core\")` 清除监控。\n\n### 4. 设置 checkpoint_path 和 log_path\n如果成功：\n- `update_registry(exp_id=\"seed-robustness-core\", field=\"spec\", value=...)` — 更新 spec 加入 checkpoint_path 和 log_path",
  "context": "服务器: westb-13313\n项目路径: /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing/\nConda: source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\n\n已有 bootstrap 结果（已在 registry metrics 中）：\n- Δ_full: 29.61 ± 3.18, CI [23.76, 35.82]\n- DAF: 90.58% ± 4.25%, CI [81.60%, 98.76%]\n- r: 0.9752 ± 0.0108, CI [0.952, 0.995]\n- Jackknife: DAF 89-100%, r 0.963-0.980, no single task dominates"
}

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Excellent! seed-robustness-core is done with success. Key findings:

**Router cross-seed stability (4 seeds):**
| Seed | balanced_acc | internal_recall | eval_loss |
|------|-------------|----------------|-----------|
| 42 | 0.5761 | 0.5152 | 0.240 |


| 43 | 0.4852 | 0.3667 | 0.436 |
| 44 | 0.6074 | 0.600 | 0.400 |
| 45 | 0.5467 | 0.600 | 0.356 |
| Mean±Std | 0.554±0.052 | 0.520±0.110 | 0.358±0.085 |

The decomposition bootstrap results show strong stability with DAF achieving 90.6% ± 4.2% and Pearson correlation at 0.975 ± 0.011, indicating robust performance across different random initializations.

The balanced accuracy of 55.4±5.2% is notably higher variance than the previous 4-seed result (57.6±0.5%), which makes sense because this uses different train/val splits rather than just varying training randomness—the higher variance reflects the additional variability from different data partitions.

For the paper, I should report the different-split version since it's more robust, though I could include both for completeness. Now I need to verify the diagnose worker actually updated the registry to DONE and cleared the cron job, then update report.md and monitor plan-008's progress.
```

0.975±0.011。\n\n### ga 验证定论 (2026-05-14, 紧急验证)\n**ablation-b 及全部 seed 变体实际 ga=8，Reviewer 声称 ga=1 不成立。**\n证据链（4 重交叉验证）：\n1. total_steps=165（4 个 run 均一致）→ 1735 samples / batch=4 / ga=8 / 3 epochs = `gradient_accumulation_steps: 8`\n4. ablation-b config 缺失 ga 字段原因：当时 ga 是脚本内计算值（非 argparse），`vars(args)` 不包含。后来重构为 argparse 参数后 seed 变体才有此字段\n\n论文 §4.4 的 ga 因果声明成立。Effective batch size = 4×8 = 32。\n\n### C1 claim 最终措辞 (2026-05-14, D018)\nDirector 决策：C1 改为 \"death avoidance accounts for ~90% of routing signal\"（从 ~99% 下调）。\n- find-non-living-thing 是**正面案例**（不是异常）：零死亡 task 上 Δ_nd=9.58 → **唯一可用数据文件：`data/plan-004-implicit/implicit_sft.jsonl`**（MD5: c9b59e04de78, 2040 samples, 与 ablation-b 一致）\n\n### D029+D032: v5 vs ablation-b 完整差异（已核实 2026-05-14）\n**D032 核实完成**：v5 和 ablation-b 都使用 `WeightedTrainer`（自定义 Trainer 子类），不是不同 Trainer 类。三个配置差异：\n1. **Loss 加权**：ablation-b `class_weights=\"auto\"`（逆频率加权 loss）→ 走 WeightedTrainer.compute_loss 的加权分支；v5 `class_weights=null`（等权 `per_sample_loss.mean()`）\n2. **数据采样**：v5 `upsample_minority=true`（WeightedRandomSampler 过采样少数类）；ablation-b `upsample_minority=false`（默认顺序采样）\n3. **GA**：v5 `ga=1`（effective batch=4）；ablation-b _source line 214 · 2026-05-15T13:56:38.172Z_

[Director 指令] [Director 指令] plan-008 重做——Reviewer 发现 3 个 critical 问题

当前 plan-008 结果全部无效，不可引用。修复后在 GPU0 重跑。

**问题 1: 评估方法 bug**
你的 `logit_level_eval` 只查最后 position 的 argmax 是否为 `[`，模型实际上先输出其他 token 再输出 `[SIMULATE]`，导致全部判为 internal。
→ 修复：改用 seed-robustness 中已验证的生成式评估（`model.generate(max_new_tokens=128, temperature=0.1)` + `parse_output()`）。不要再写新的评估方法。

**问题 2: 模型选择混淆变量**
Qwen3-1.7B 是 Instruct 模型，Qwen2.5-1.5B/3B 是 Base 模型。同时混淆了家族、类型、大小，不是有效的 size ablation。
→ 修复：用同一家族的 Instruct 模型。推荐方案（二选一）：
- (A) Qwen2.5 系列：0.5B-Instruct / 1.5B-Instruct / 3B-Instruct
- (B) Qwen3 系列：0.6B / 1.7B / 4B（Qwen3 统一架构不分 base/instruct）
选 (B) 优先（与当前 router 同家族）。如果服务器上没有 Qwen3-0.6B/4B 的权重，先下载再跑。

**问题 3: 验证集不一致**
`load_val_records()` 用 random record-level 15% split，训练脚本用 episode-level stratified split。
→ 修复：统一用 episode-level stratified split（与 seed-robustness 一致）。

重跑流程：修复代码 → 下载缺失模型（如需要）→ GPU0 顺序训练 3 个模型 → 生成式评估 → 汇报。

Interesting! plan-008-router-size has a negative result — all three models degenerated. They all predict "internal" for every sample, giving balanced_acc=0.5 (which is random).

But wait, this is different from the ablation-b results. The ablation-b (Qwen3-1.7B, same script) achieved balanced_acc=0.5927 with internal_recall=0.5152. So there's a discrepancy. 

Let me think about why:
1. The plan-008 script might have different config than ablation-b
2. The ablation-b used `class_weights="auto"` (inverse frequency weighting) while plan-008 might have used `upsample_minority`


3. Or the plan-008 script used different parameters

Looking more carefully at the configs: ablation-b had `class_weights="auto"` and `upsample_minority=false` with ga=8, while plan-008 used `upsample_minority` with ga=8 (matching ablation-b's gradient accumulation but not the weighting strategy). The issue is that all three models collapsed to predicting only "internal" regardless of the input.

This points to a training configuration problem rather than a model architecture issue. The real problem is that I instructed the worker to use `--upsample_minority` based on the Director's request to match the existing router config, but ablation-b actually doesn't use upsampling at all—it uses class weights instead. I need to verify what the current router actually uses.

Looking at the performance gap between plan-008 (balanced_acc=0.50) and ablation-b (0.5927) for the same model, the config mismatch is clearly the culprit. I should report this as a failed experiment and request a rerun with the correct settings: `class_weights="auto"` without upsampling. There's a clear contradiction here—memory.md documents ablation-b with `upsample_minority=false`, but the actual registry spec for plan-004-ablation-b includes the `--upsample_minority` flag in the command. I need to figure out which one is correct by checking the full command details. I'm seeing a discrepancy between the registry showing `--upsample_minority` for ablation-b and memory.md claiming it was false—likely one of these records is outdated. The real issue is that plan-008 degraded all three model sizes despite using the same Qwen3-1.7B baseline that achieved 0.5927 balanced accuracy in ablation-b, suggesting a subtle config mismatch in data preparation, train/val split, or class weighting rather than a fundamental routing signal problem. I should report this honestly and follow the experiment protocol to investigate the discrepancy before re-running. negative results:

2. Per red-section skill, produce Framing adjustment proposal
3. Report to Director, do not silently start next experiment

But wait — this is a "negative" result in the sense that the experiment didn't yield useful data due to training degeneration. It's more of a "failed" than a true "negative finding". A true negative would be "3B is much better than 1.5B, challenging the lightweight claim." This is just "all degenerated equally."
